**[00:04:17.960] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000052)  
On behalf of the Governance Commission for GOCCs or the GCG,

**[00:04:23.720] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000053)  
we express our sincere appreciation

**[00:04:25.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000054)  
for the opportunity to present our accomplishments,

**[00:04:29.340] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000055)  
ongoing initiatives and budget proposal for fiscal year 2027,

**[00:04:34.700] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000056)  
as well as our accomplishments and ongoing initiatives

**[00:04:40.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000057)  
that were supported by the previous year's budgets.

**[00:04:43.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000058)  
We hope to show how the reforms implemented in the GOCC sector

**[00:04:49.340] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000059)  
continue to generate stronger governance,

**[00:04:52.340] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000060)  
better performance,

**[00:04:54.340] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000061)  
and greater returns to government.

**[00:04:55.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000062)  
As well as how our proposed budget will enable us to sustain these gains.

**[00:05:02.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000063)  
As President Ferdinand R. Marcos Jr. aptly emphasized,

**[00:05:06.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000064)  
and I quote,

**[00:05:07.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000065)  
we have a duty to uphold the integrity of our institutions

**[00:05:11.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000066)  
by demanding transparency, accountability,

**[00:05:15.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000067)  
and a standard of service worthy of the Filipino people.

**[00:05:19.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000068)  
This principle lies at the very heart of GCG's mandate.

**[00:05:24.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000069)  
The values of transparency, accountability,

**[00:05:27.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000070)  
and public service excellence

**[00:05:29.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000071)  
are the standards that guide our oversight

**[00:05:32.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000072)  
of government-owned or controlled corporations

**[00:05:35.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000073)  
and shape the reforms we pursue across the GOCC sector.

**[00:05:41.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000074)  
Before discussing our accomplishments and budget proposal,

**[00:05:45.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000075)  
allow me to briefly highlight the mandate of the GCG.

**[00:05:49.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000076)  
Our mandate provides the context for the programs and reforms

**[00:05:53.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000077)  
that we implement across the GOCC sector.

**[00:05:57.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000078)  
It also illustrates how our work in the GCG

**[00:06:00.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000079)  
contributes to good governance,

**[00:06:03.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000080)  
fiscal responsibility,

**[00:06:05.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000081)  
and improved public service delivery.

**[00:06:09.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000082)  
The GCG was established through Republic Act No. 10149,

**[00:06:14.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000083)  
otherwise known as the GOCC Governance Act of 2011.

**[00:06:19.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000084)  
The law was enacted to strengthen oversight

**[00:06:22.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000085)  
of GOCCs

**[00:06:24.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000086)  
and to ensure that these corporations

**[00:06:26.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000087)  
are managed responsibly, professionally,

**[00:06:30.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000088)  
and in a financially sustainable manner.

**[00:06:35.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000089)  
Through its mandate,

**[00:06:37.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000090)  
the GCG serves as the central oversight body

**[00:06:40.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000091)  
that promotes fiscal discipline, accountability,

**[00:06:44.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000092)  
transparency, and sound corporate governance

**[00:06:47.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000093)  
throughout the GOCC sector,

**[00:06:50.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000094)  
helping translate the President's vision

**[00:06:53.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000095)  
of trustworthy, high-performing public institutions

**[00:06:57.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000096)  
into concrete outcomes for the Filipino people.

**[00:07:04.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000097)  
The GCG performs four core functions.

**[00:07:08.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000098)  
First, we rationalize the sector

**[00:07:11.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000099)  
by determining whether GOCCs continue to serve their intended purpose

**[00:07:16.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000100)  
and whether they should be reorganized, merged,

**[00:07:19.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000101)  
privatized, or abolished.

**[00:07:22.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000102)  
Second, we ensure that qualified and competent individuals

**[00:07:26.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000103)  
serve on GOCC boards.

**[00:07:29.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000104)  
Third, we evaluate institutional and individual performance

**[00:07:34.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000105)  
through standardized performance assessment systems.

**[00:07:38.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000106)  
And fourth, we establish compensation and position classification policies

**[00:07:43.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000107)  
that promote fairness, competitiveness, and accountability.

**[00:07:49.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000108)  
Next slide.

**[00:07:50.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000109)

the GCG supervises 112 GOCCs across nine sectors.

**[00:09:52.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000142)

**[00:09:58.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000143)

**[00:10:02.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000144)

**[00:10:06.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000145)

**[00:10:18.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000149)

**[00:11:46.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000174)  
From 2023 to 2025,

**[00:11:49.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000175)  
we see a consistent decline in total subsidies

**[00:11:52.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000176)  
received by the GOCC sector.

**[00:11:55.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000177)  
Subsidies went down from 154 billion in 2023

**[00:12:00.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000178)  
to 128 billion in 2024

**[00:12:04.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000179)  
and 96 billion in 2025.

**[00:12:07.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000180)  
This represents a reduction of about

**[00:12:09.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000181)  
38% over the past three years.

**[00:12:13.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000182)  
In 2025,

**[00:12:15.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000183)  
the total assets of the GOCC sector

**[00:12:18.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000184)  
was recorded at 14.38 trillion pesos,

**[00:12:25.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000185)  
trillion, trillion,

**[00:12:27.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000186)  
trillion pesos, Your Honor,

**[00:12:29.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000187)  
which exhibited an overall increase of 8%

**[00:12:32.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000188)  
from the previous year.

**[00:12:49.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000197)  
After the establishment of the GCG,

**[00:12:52.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000198)  
GOCC dividends significantly increased

**[00:12:54.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000199)  
throughout the years.

**[00:12:56.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000200)  
For comparison,

**[00:12:58.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000201)  
from 2002 to 2011,

**[00:13:02.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000202)  
or before the GCG was established,

**[00:13:05.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000203)  
the total dividend remittances of GOCCs was

**[00:13:07.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000204)  
68.71 billion,

**[00:13:11.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000205)  
with an average of 6.87 billion annually.

**[00:13:16.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000206)  
After the establishment of the GCG,

**[00:13:19.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000207)  
the total dividends is,

**[00:13:21.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000208)  
as of today,

**[00:13:23.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000209)  
is 626.62 billion,

**[00:13:26.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000210)  
with a 44 billion annual average.

**[00:13:32.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000211)  
Before we go into details,

**[00:13:34.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000212)  
allow me to present the GCG's proposed

**[00:13:37.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000213)  
2027 budget.

**[00:13:40.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000214)  
The budget is designed to support

**[00:13:42.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000215)  
the Commission's programs and priorities,

**[00:13:45.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000216)  
ensuring that we can continue delivering

**[00:13:47.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000217)  
on our mandates.

**[00:13:49.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000218)  
For the proposed budget,

**[00:13:51.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000219)  
the GCG has aligned its funding requirements

**[00:13:54.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000220)  
with its Tier 1 agency priorities,

**[00:13:57.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000221)  
which represent the most critical initiatives.

**[00:14:02.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000222)  
This focuses on implementing and reviewing

**[00:14:06.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000223)  
the Compensation and Position Classification System 2

**[00:14:10.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000224)  
in preparation for the regular review

**[00:14:14.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000225)  
of the CPCS,

**[00:14:16.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000226)  
in preparation for CPCS 3,

**[00:14:19.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000227)  
hopefully in 2028 or 2027.

**[00:14:24.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000228)  
The review will start in 2027

**[00:14:27.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000229)  
and for implementation in 2028.

**[00:14:31.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000230)  
Enhancing the integrated corporate reporting system

**[00:14:35.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000231)  
and the performance evaluation system

**[00:14:38.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000232)  
or the PES,

**[00:14:39.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000233)  
identifying and evaluating GOCCs

**[00:14:42.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000234)  
for possible dispositive action,

**[00:14:44.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000235)  
strengthening the WHIP

**[00:14:46.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000236)  
or the Whistleblowing and Integrity Portal program,

**[00:14:50.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000237)  
sustaining the talent pool for appointed positions,

**[00:14:53.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000238)  
supporting the liquidation of GOCCs

**[00:14:56.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000239)  
approved for abolition,

**[00:14:58.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000240)  
and

**[00:15:00.000] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000241)  
enhancing the Corporate Governance Scorecard, and pursuing organizational structuring plans for GOCCs.

**[00:15:07.780] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000242)  
In addition to its Tier 1 priorities, the proposed additional budget will also support GCG's Tier 2 initiatives,

**[00:15:17.200] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000243)  
aimed at sustaining and enhancing its long-term capabilities.

**[00:15:21.600] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000244)  
These include fleet modernization to improve operational mobility,

**[00:15:25.640] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000245)  
the continued enhancement of the enhanced ICRS, the digital transformation initiatives.

**[00:15:38.080] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000246)  
The budget will likewise support organizational expansion requirements,

**[00:15:43.760] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000247)  
institutionalization of ESG and sustainability initiatives in line with OECD and international standards,

**[00:15:51.800] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000248)  
and the development of strategic local and international initiatives.

**[00:15:55.640] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000249)  
The budget will also support international partnerships to elevate governance standards across the GOCC sector.

**[00:16:02.840] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000250)  
For 2027, the GCG has been allocated 408.382 million pesos,

**[00:16:12.260] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000251)  
which accounts for 171 filled plantilla positions, or P197.920 million.

**[00:16:23.380] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000252)  
P174.389 million

**[00:16:25.640] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000253)  
of maintenance and operating expenses, and P36.073 million of capital outlay.

.852 million already obligated.

Maintenance and other operating expenses accounts for P188.532 million

When

**[00:18:25.640] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000278)  
accounting for external budget adjustments, specifically adding the performance-based bonus release of P0.922 million and deducting the P6.286 million in savings generated from National Budget Circular 602, our final net fiscal year 2026 GCG budget total stands at P389.185 million.

**[00:18:54.140] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000279)  
With total obligations reaching P218.322 million, our net budget utilization rate steps up to 56.10% as we head into the final third of the fiscal year.

**[00:19:12.100] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000280)  
This multi-year trend highlights the GECG's budget trajectory, anchored by two major funding spikes directly tied to the compensation and position classification system.

**[00:19:24.140] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000281)  
The first spike in 2019, funding rose sharply to $258.2 million for the initial implementation, maintaining a strong 80% utilization rate.

**[00:19:45.920] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000282)  
The second spike in 2023 for the second review, allocation peaked at an all-time high of P421.6 million.

**[00:19:54.140] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000284)  
This comprehensive review tested our absorption capacity. The true success story follows those spikes. [laughter] As funding normalized in 2024 and 2025, we achieved operational efficiency, driving our budget utilization rates up to 88.93% and 90.59%.

**[00:20:24.420] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000287)  
Currently, for 2026, we stand at $394.5 million, with utilization tracking steadily at 51.56% as of mid-year.

**[00:20:39.860] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000288)  
Moving into 2027, the proposed $408.3 million NEP ceiling ensures we sustain this momentum and continue our rigorous oversight mandates.

**[00:21:15.480] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000292)

**[00:21:25.880] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000293)

**[00:21:33.600] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000294)

**[00:21:38.520] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000295)

**[00:21:42.220] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000296)  
Additional MOOE funding,

**[00:21:44.680] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000297)

**[00:21:53.120] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000298)

**[00:22:00.540] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000299)

**[00:22:08.160] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000300)  
that have exceeded their useful life,

**[00:22:11.200] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000301)  
with our oldest vehicle now running for almost 13 years.

**[00:22:15.660] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000302)  
Enhancement of major ICT projects,

**[00:22:19.700] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000303)  
169,904,000.

**[00:22:24.200] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000304)  
Our largest investment covering both MOOE and capital outlay

**[00:22:30.180] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000305)  
to procure critical ICT equipment,

**[00:22:34.320] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000306)  
scale up services and infrastructure,

**[00:22:36.960] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000307)  
and reinforce our cybersecurity defenses.

**[00:22:41.120] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000308)  
Following the approval of the GCG's

**[00:22:44.680] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000309)  
revised organizational restructuring

**[00:22:46.480] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000310)  
and staffing pattern,

An additional budgetary allocation under its maintenance and other operating expenses

**[00:22:53.140] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000312)  
in the amount of P25,942,000 is formally requested.

**[00:23:00.020] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000313)  
The approved restructuring,

**[00:23:01.980] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000314)  
which expands the headcount from 239 to 292 positions,

**[00:23:07.860] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000315)

**[00:23:14.680] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000317)

**[00:23:31.480] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000321)

**[00:23:41.320] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000323)

**[00:23:55.960] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000327)

Next slide, please.

team, form part

and support the DICT's

cyber security.

In support of the DVM-approved

**[00:29:14.120] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000477)

and staffing pattern

**[00:29:17.000] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000479)  
and the GCG's continuing

**[00:29:19.180] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000480)  
workforce expansion,

**[00:29:21.440] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000481)  
the procurement of additional

**[00:29:22.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000482)  
laptops and ICT equipment,

**[00:29:25.500] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000483)  
including the replacement of

**[00:29:27.120] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000484)  
obsolete and unserviceable units,

**[00:29:30.080] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000485)  
is critical to sustaining

**[00:29:31.480] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000486)  
operational efficiency across

**[00:29:33.360] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000487)  
all three sites.

**[00:29:35.220] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000488)  
The project will equip personnel

**[00:29:37.240] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000489)  
with reliable computing resources,

**[00:29:39.220] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000490)  
software subscriptions,

**[00:29:42.380] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000491)  
collaboration platforms,

**[00:29:43.860] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000492)  
and other essential ICT tools

**[00:29:45.980] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000493)  
to enhance productivity,

**[00:29:48.800] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000494)  
strengthen institutional

**[00:29:49.960] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000495)  
capacity, and ensure

**[00:29:52.220] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000496)  
the effective delivery of the

**[00:29:54.020] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000497)  
GCG's regulatory

**[00:29:55.840] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000498)  
oversight and governance

**[00:29:58.420] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000499)  
mandates.

**[00:30:00.000] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000500)  
Many of our existing laptops and ICT equipment are already obsolete, defective, or beyond their useful life.

**[00:30:09.120] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000501)  
Continued reliance on these devices affects productivity.

**[00:30:13.420] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000502)  
One of the most significant risks faced by any digital organization is the loss of critical systems during a disaster or major disruption.

**[00:30:23.080] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000503)  
At present, the GCG does not have its own disaster recovery facility and remains dependent on shared resources outside the agency.

**[00:30:36.180] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000504)  
Establishing a dedicated disaster recovery site will ensure the continued availability of critical systems and minimize downtime of digital services relied upon by the GOCC sector and the general public.

**[00:30:53.080] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000505)  
This initiative aims to enhance and sustain the GCG main office data center infrastructure to ensure the continuous, secure, and reliable delivery of ICT services.

**[00:31:07.540] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000506)  
Recent incidents, including power infrastructure failure and water leakage from malfunctioning air conditioning units, have highlighted the need to improve the data center's resiliency, reliability, and business continuity

**[00:31:23.160] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000507)  
capabilities to support the Commission's expanding digital operations.

**[00:31:30.300] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000508)  
Recent incidents, including the interrupted power source overheating and water leakages within the data center, have highlighted vulnerabilities that could potentially affect critical ICT infrastructure and services.

**[00:31:48.480] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000509)  
Much of the ICT infrastructure supporting our head office operations

**[00:31:53.080] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000510)  
is already approaching or has reached end-of-life status.

**[00:31:58.880] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000511)  
Unsupported network equipment increases the risk of system failures,

**[00:32:04.280] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000512)  
cybersecurity vulnerabilities, and service interruptions.

**[00:32:09.120] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000513)  
Similar investments are also necessary for the ICT infrastructure

**[00:32:13.660] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000514)  
deployed at the Extension Office of the GCG, the Extension Office A.

**[00:32:19.040] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000515)  
3. Existing facilities, including air conditioning units

**[00:32:23.360] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000516)  
that are essential for maintaining data center operations,

**[00:32:27.980] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000517)  
have experienced breakdowns that affect the reliability of ICT services.

**[00:32:34.920] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000518)  
As we conclude our presentations, Your Honors,

**[00:32:38.480] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000519)  
allow us to leave one final perspective.

**[00:32:41.780] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000520)  
The GCG is requesting a proposed fiscal year 2027 budget

**[00:32:47.180] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000521)  
of P602.40

**[00:32:49.040] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000522)  
million, including both Tier 1 and Tier 2.

**[00:32:54.180] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000523)  
Relative to the fiscal year 2026 budget of 6.79 trillion,

**[00:33:01.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000524)  
this represents only 0.0089% of the total government appropriations.

**[00:33:08.940] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000525)  
Nevertheless, with this modest share of the national budget,

**[00:33:13.460] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000526)  
the GCG performs oversight over a GOC sector

**[00:33:16.700] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000527)  
that manages trillions.

**[00:33:19.040] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000528)  
In addition, the GCG is also responsible for the financial and public assets,

**[00:33:23.140] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000529)  
generates revenues for government,

**[00:33:25.520] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000530)  
and contributes to substantial dividend remittances to the national treasury.

**[00:33:30.020] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000531)  
The resources entrusted to us are only a small fraction of the value

**[00:33:35.180] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000532)  
we help protect, monitor, and optimize on behalf of the Filipino people.

**[00:33:41.420] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000533)  
Through our governance reforms, performance management, fiscal oversight, digital transformation, and

**[00:33:49.040] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000534)  
accountability measures, the GCG helps ensure that GOCCs remain financially sound

**[00:33:57.500] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000535)  
and responsive to national development policies.

**[00:34:02.340] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000536)  
Our proposed budget is an investment that enables the Governance Commission

**[00:34:06.600] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000537)  
to continue safeguarding public resources and maximizing the value that GOCCs deliver to the Filipino people.

**[00:34:16.140] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000538)  
Guided by the vision of our President,

**[00:34:19.040] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000539)  
the GCG remains committed to strengthening governance across the sector

**[00:34:23.800] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000540)  
and ensuring that every GOCC contributes meaningfully to nation-building.

**[00:34:29.820] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000541)  
With your continued support, Your Honors, we can sustain these gains

**[00:34:33.120] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000542)  
and further advance a GOCC sector that delivers greater value,

**[00:34:38.700] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000543)  
greater accountability, and greater service to the Filipino people.

**[00:34:43.000] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000544)  
For these reasons, we respectfully seek the favorable consideration

**[00:34:47.220] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000545)  
of the honorable

**[00:34:49.040] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000554)  
subcommittee for the GCG's

**[00:34:51.160] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000555)  
fiscal year 2027 proposed budget.

**[00:34:55.760] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000556)  
Thank you very much, Your Honor.

**[00:34:57.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000557)  
Thank you, Attorney Corpus.

**[00:34:58.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000558)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:34:59.820] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000559)

**[00:35:03.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000560)

**[00:35:07.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000561)  
Of the 113 GOCCs in your purview, is that the correct word?

**[00:35:15.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000562)

**[00:35:17.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000563)  
In your stable.

**[00:35:19.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000564)

**[00:35:26.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000565)  
Neto, Net.

**[00:35:28.600] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000566)

**[00:35:33.980] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000567)  
Because GOCC, specifically if I recall right, the Philippine Aerospace Development Corporation

**[00:35:42.480] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000568)  
had expired, its existence had expired early this year.

**[00:35:50.160] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000569)

**[00:35:54.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000570)

**[00:36:01.460] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000571)  
Yes, we have the list Your Honor, I don't have it with me right now.

**[00:36:06.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000572)  
Anybody can answer? Director Kenneth, for the record.

**[00:36:11.060] GCG Director Kenneth M. Rafols** (asr-000573)  
Hello, good afternoon.

**[00:36:12.480] GCG Director Kenneth M. Rafols** (asr-000574)  
As of 2025, 43 GOCCs remits dividends, then 27 is subsidized po.

**[00:36:22.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000575)  
Could you, like which GOCCs would have the biggest subsidies?

**[00:36:28.400] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000576)  
Just for the record lang para alam rin namin ni Chairperson JV.

**[00:36:35.560] GCG Director Kenneth M. Rafols** (asr-000577)  
For 2025, Senator, it's PhilHealth po.

**[00:36:39.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000578)  
PhilHealth has the biggest subsidy?

**[00:36:41.520] GCG Director Kenneth M. Rafols** (asr-000579)  
Yes po.

**[00:36:42.080] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000580)  
And what else pa?

**[00:36:44.180] GCG Director Kenneth M. Rafols** (asr-000581)  
And National Irrigation Administration, or NIA po.

**[00:36:48.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000582)  
Okay.

**[00:36:49.400] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000583)  
And tama ba, Attorney Corpus, is it the goal of all GOCCs to eventually be net positive

**[00:36:58.440] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000584)  
and remitting to the government or not necessarily?

**[00:37:01.540] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000585)  
Not necessarily, Your Honor, because there are certain GOCCs that have mandates that cater directly to the public

**[00:37:09.780] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000586)  
and for this they would need...

**[00:37:12.080] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000587)  
Yes po.

**[00:37:13.080] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000588)  
...subsidies from the national government, but we also monitor their performance in terms

**[00:37:17.980] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000589)  
of their utilization of the subsidy allocated to them, if it is effective as far as...

**[00:37:25.020] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000590)  
Could you give us some examples, Attorney Corpus?

**[00:37:27.560] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000591)  
Yes, the National Food Authority.

**[00:37:31.880] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000592)  
Can we have the National?

**[00:37:34.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000593)  
Anyone from your team can answer.

**[00:37:48.240] GCG Corporate Standards Office Director IV Atty. Jocelyn Grace N. Navato** (asr-000597)  
Yeah. For NFA po, for 2025, it received a subsidy of 14 billion, but it's performing its social

**[00:37:57.960] GCG Corporate Standards Office Director IV Atty. Jocelyn Grace N. Navato** (asr-000598)  
mandate of buffer stocking.

**[00:38:01.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000599)  
Well, we know that there were some issues with the NFA after the rice tarification law,

**[00:38:06.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000600)  
di ba?

**[00:38:08.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000601)  
In any case,

**[00:38:09.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000602)  
you assess every

**[00:38:11.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000603)  
GOCC.

**[00:38:12.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000604)  
Some have a clear mandate that they should be remitting, and some, their mandate is based on their charter or based on the law that created them.

**[00:38:22.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000605)  
So you judge them differently.

**[00:38:24.420] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000606)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[00:38:24.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000607)  
So it's not necessarily true that your ultimate indicator is how much you remit.

**[00:38:30.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000608)  
Kasi usually, pag-GOCC, ang tinitinan ng tao, magkano ba yung nababalik sa gobyerno.

**[00:38:36.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000609)  
Tama ba yun, Atty. Marius?

**[00:38:37.480] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000610)  
Yes, Yes, Your Honor.

**[00:38:38.280] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000611)  
Then for the record, Your Honor, as far as the National Food Authority is concerned, their performance is actually improving for the past two years.

**[00:38:50.700] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000612)  
There's an upward turn in their performance rating.

**[00:38:54.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000613)  
When we say GOCC, it has the nature of a corporation, so they're allowed to engage in business, correct?

**[00:39:02.920] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000614)  
Our GOCC is allowed to engage in commercial operations, Your Honor.

**[00:39:07.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000615)  
Yes.

**[00:39:08.280] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000616)  
But it's not necessarily the case that all GOCCs are expected to transact or expected to do commercial transactions.

**[00:39:16.900] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000617)  
Not necessarily.

**[00:39:17.280] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000618)  
In fact, the classification of GOCCs are categorized into three.

**[00:39:23.680] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000619)  
Those that are subsidized by the national government, that those that are performing public functions but are allowed to engage in commercial activities,

**[00:39:33.260] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000620)  
and those that are engaged in commercial activities in competition

**[00:39:38.280] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000621)  
with the private sector.

**[00:39:40.080] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000622)  
So, we saw in your presentation that over the past 12 years, the remittances have grown higher, correct?

**[00:39:50.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000623)  
Which is, of course, an accomplishment.

**[00:39:52.580] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000624)  
But, siguro if we leave that as our only or as our main metric, it will create the impression that all GOCCs need to remit, which is not necessarily the case.

**[00:40:02.760] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000625)  
Sure.

**[00:40:02.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000626)  
So, in your assessment of the 113, now 112 GOCCs,

**[00:40:08.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000627)

**[00:40:13.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000628)

**[00:40:21.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000630)

**[00:40:30.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000631)

**[00:40:32.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000632)

**[00:40:33.860] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000633)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[00:40:35.040] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000634)

**[00:40:38.280] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000635)  
unfortunately, there are some GOCCs that are, you know, not really performing up to par.

**[00:40:45.880] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000636)

**[00:41:00.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000637)

greater improvement. Para lang for our appreciation also.

**[00:41:41.620] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000648)

**[00:41:42.860] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000649)

**[00:41:47.620] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000650)

**[00:41:54.080] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000651)

**[00:41:59.100] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000652)

**[00:42:21.260] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000658)

**[00:42:25.860] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000659)

**[00:42:32.740] GCG Chairperson Atty. Marius P. Corpus** (asr-000660)

**[01:06:29.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001843)

**[01:06:38.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001844)

**[01:06:43.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001845)

**[01:06:47.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001846)

**[01:06:50.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001847)

**[01:06:52.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001848)

**[01:06:58.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001849)  
So, that's part of my presentation.

**[01:07:00.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001850)  
Mr. Chair, with that, I fully support ang budget ng DTI.

**[01:07:04.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001851)  
Alang-alang sa mga consumer.

**[01:07:05.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001852)  
Okay, salamat, Senator.

**[01:07:07.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001853)  
Thank you, Senator.

**[01:07:08.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001854)  
Okay.

**[01:07:11.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001855)  
Lalakas ng palakpak, Senator, oh.

**[01:07:13.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001856)  
Lalakas ng palakpak.

**[01:07:14.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001857)  
Okay.

**[01:07:16.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001858)  
Secretary, I'm gonna put you on the spot.

**[01:07:18.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001859)  
Yung Trustmark.

**[01:07:19.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001860)  
As you know, I also had some comments about the Trustmark.

**[01:07:21.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001861)  
I also had some comments about the Trustmark previously.

**[01:07:25.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001862)  
We don't want to create another layer.

**[01:07:28.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001863)  
That's why the suggestion ni Sen. Tulfo, in which you also suggested before,

**[01:07:32.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001864)  
is huwag na siyang mandatory for the small.

**[01:07:35.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001865)  
If you want to do it mandatory for the medium and large,

**[01:07:39.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001866)  
kayang-kaya nila yung 1,000 pesos.

**[01:07:41.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001867)  
But ang worry namin is para sa small,

**[01:07:43.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001868)  
firstly, it's an extra expense.

**[01:07:45.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001869)  
And secondly, it's an extra step.

**[01:07:47.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001870)  
Eh, gusto nga natin bawasan yung mga steps

**[01:07:49.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001871)  
para mas madali magdegosyo.

**[01:07:51.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001872)  
Eventually, I think people will understand that

**[01:07:54.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001873)  
if there's a Trustmark, they can trust the product more.

**[01:07:57.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001874)  
Pero that can be, tama ba caveat emptor na yan, diba?

**[01:08:01.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001875)  
It's already the purview of the buyer.

**[01:08:04.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001876)  
Tama ba? Tama ba yung Latin ko?

**[01:08:05.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001877)  
Caveat emptor, tama diba?

**[01:08:06.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001878)  
Bahala na yung buyer.

**[01:08:08.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001879)  
So, when will you release the DO?

**[01:08:14.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001880)  
Because your current DO says that it will be mandatory next year.

**[01:08:18.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001881)  
I hope by the time we get to plenary,

**[01:08:21.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001882)  
there's already more visibility

**[01:08:24.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001883)  
that we won't make it mandatory anymore

**[01:08:27.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001884)  
for the small and the micro.

**[01:08:29.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001885)  
That's our suggestion, Secretary.

**[01:08:31.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001886)  
So, pag nagkita tayo sa plenary,

**[01:08:33.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001887)  
I hope we can resolve that already.

**[01:08:38.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001888)  
The more steps kasi we have,

**[01:08:40.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001889)  
it's really going to be difficult.

**[01:08:42.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001890)  
Plus, ito magiging problema ni ASEC.

**[01:08:45.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001891)  
Let's say, bumili ako ng Trustmark.

**[01:08:49.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001892)  
Talaga bang ma-assure natin na just because bumili ako ng Trustmark,

**[01:08:53.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001893)  
ay katiwa-tiwala na yung produktong yan.

**[01:08:55.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001894)  
Kailangan malinaw na malinaw na pag may Trustmark ako,

**[01:08:59.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001895)  
that it's really above the rest of the market.

**[01:09:04.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001896)  
And I'm not sure if you can assure that, diba?

**[01:09:06.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001897)  
So, may ano rin yan.

**[01:09:08.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001898)  
May possible pitfall rin yun yung pagkakaroon ng Trustmark.

**[01:09:11.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001899)  
Kung yung medium, kaya nyo talagang ma-assess yan isa-isa.

**[01:09:15.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001900)  
Pero kung yung small, papatrustmark ninyo,

**[01:09:17.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001901)  
kaya nyo ba talagang i-assess lahat ng libo-libong mag-a-apply sa inyo?

**[01:09:23.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001902)  
So, that's just some possible pitfalls moving forward.

**[01:09:26.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001903)  
So, I would suggest, bigyan nyo ng Trustmark,

**[01:09:29.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001904)  
yung talagang kaya nyo sabihin na this product is really top standard,

**[01:09:35.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001905)  
hindi nito masisira at lahat-lahat.

**[01:09:37.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001906)  
And again, if it's for medium or even the larger enterprises, big purchases,

**[01:09:43.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001907)  
then why not?

**[01:09:44.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001908)  
Pero for the small, yung mga produktong nabibili natin,

**[01:09:49.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001909)  
parang almost impulse purchase na yung mga yan,

**[01:09:52.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001910)  
I would tread carefully.

**[01:09:54.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001911)  
Kasi once you have that Trustmark,

**[01:09:56.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001912)  
you have to assure also that lahat yan will fall under the right standards.

**[01:10:01.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001913)  
In my mind, considering yung dami ninyo at yung dami ng mga negosyo at dami ng produkto,

**[01:10:06.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001914)  
parang hindi talaga siya nagmamatch.

**[01:10:09.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001915)  
So, I hope you can work with the Secretary on that.

**[01:10:11.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001916)  
Ang suggestion ko is,

**[01:10:12.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001917)  
maganda na yung direction,

**[01:10:14.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001918)  
keep it to the bigger businesses,

**[01:10:16.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001919)  
and then yung mga smaller businesses,

**[01:10:18.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001920)  
let it be voluntary on their part.

**[01:10:20.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001921)  
Do you want to respond, Sir Asec?

**[01:10:22.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001922)  
Baka may mga blind spot kami hindi nakikita.

**[01:10:25.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001923)  
Go ahead.

**[01:10:26.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001924)  
Tama po yun, Senator.

**[01:10:28.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001925)  
We will comply with that.

**[01:10:30.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001926)  
I totally agree with you on this.

**[01:10:33.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001927)  
Salamat.

**[01:10:34.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001928)  
Sir, you want to respond?

**[01:10:36.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001929)  
Yun na yun?

**[01:10:37.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001930)  
Yun na yun?

**[01:10:38.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001931)  
Okay, sige.

**[01:10:39.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001932)  
So, let's begin.

**[01:10:40.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001933)  
Secretary, you have the floor.

**[01:10:41.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001934)  
Hopefully, we can keep it to maybe 15 minutes, no?

**[01:10:45.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001935)  
Yeah.

**[01:10:46.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001936)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair and members of the committee.

**[01:10:50.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001937)  
For fiscal year 2027,

**[01:10:53.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001938)  
the proposed budget of the Department of Trade and Industry is 10.903 billion,

**[01:10:59.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001939)  
composed of 7 billion for DTI, OSEC,

**[01:11:03.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001940)  
and 2.131 billion for attached agencies,

**[01:11:07.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001941)  
and 1.7363 billion for attached corporations.

**[01:11:13.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001942)  
This budget supports DTI's priorities.

**[01:11:16.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001943)  
So, we have three priorities.

**[01:11:18.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001944)  
Expand foreign trade and investments,

**[01:11:20.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001945)  
protect consumers and ensure fair trade,

**[01:11:23.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001946)  
and develop competitive MSMEs and local industries.

**[01:11:27.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001947)  
For the performance for 2025 and fiscal year 2026,

**[01:11:33.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001948)  
first semester,

**[01:11:35.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001949)  
DTI…

**[01:11:36.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001950)  
allow me to highlight our major achievements in these three areas.

**[01:11:40.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001951)  
First, on trade and investments,

**[01:11:42.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001952)  
Philippine exports reached 135.93 billion U.S. dollars in 2025,

**[01:11:49.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001953)  
composed of 84.44 billion in goods export

**[01:11:53.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001954)  
and 51.49 billion U.S. dollars in services exports.

**[01:11:59.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001955)  
For 2026,

**[01:12:00.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001956)  
the momentum continued with goods export reaching 54.92 billion U.S. dollars,

**[01:12:06.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001957)  
as of July 2026,

**[01:12:08.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001958)  
while services reached 23.31 billion U.S. dollars as of June 2026.

**[01:12:14.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001959)  
In 2025, Philippine exports reached all-time high, 15.4%.

**[01:12:20.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001960)  
We carried this momentum straight in 2026

**[01:12:23.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001961)  
with monthly exports growing at January 8.7,

**[01:12:27.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001962)  
February 8.9%,

**[01:12:29.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001963)  
March 20.4%,

**[01:12:31.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001964)  
April 7.2%,

**[01:12:33.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001965)  
May 7.6,

**[01:12:35.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001966)  
June 24.1,

**[01:12:37.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001967)  
July 10.8,

**[01:12:38.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001968)  
and August is up,

**[01:12:39.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001969)  
but PCA will announce tomorrow the exact figure.

**[01:12:43.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001970)  
Semiconductors, electronic, automotive parts, and minerals

**[01:12:47.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001971)  
continue to account for a significant share of our exports,

**[01:12:50.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001972)  
but we have seen a lot of wins in the agriculture exports,

**[01:12:54.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001973)  
particularly coconut, banana, pineapples, ube, and calamansi.

**[01:12:58.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001974)  
But we are pushing also for bamboo.

**[01:13:02.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001975)  
Just this year,

**[01:13:03.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001976)  
we also sent our first shipments

**[01:13:04.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001977)  
of fresh mangoes to Canada.

**[01:13:08.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001978)  
400 kilos we started a month ago,

**[01:13:11.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001979)  
and then last two weeks,

**[01:13:13.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001980)  
we shipped also one metric tons of fresh mangoes,

**[01:13:17.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001981)  
and then we'll see how it goes.

**[01:13:19.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001982)  
We also shipped 150 kilos to Brussels, Europe,

**[01:13:23.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001983)  
and now we have an inquiry from UAE

**[01:13:27.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001984)  
for two metric tons of fresh mangoes.

**[01:13:31.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001985)  
So again, durian mangosteen also to Canada,

**[01:13:33.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001986)  
and we also had it.

**[01:13:35.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001987)  
Then the success of our exporters creates opportunities

**[01:13:39.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001988)  
and income for farmers,

**[01:13:41.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001989)  
creates jobs in rural communities,

**[01:13:43.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001990)  
and strengthens local supply chains across the country.

**[01:13:47.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001991)  
For example,

**[01:13:48.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001992)  
one exporter supported by DTI

**[01:13:51.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001993)  
has expanded the business from 2023 to last year

**[01:13:55.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001994)  
by times four during this administration.

**[01:13:58.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001995)  
So to meet the growing demand,

**[01:14:00.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001996)  
the company sources an average of 600 metric tons

**[01:14:03.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001997)  
of agriculture imports per month

**[01:14:06.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001998)  
from farmers and communities,

**[01:14:08.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-001999)  
and they deliver on a daily basis

**[01:14:10.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002000)  
these small enterprises.

**[01:14:12.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002001)  
So we encourage the small enterprises

**[01:14:15.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002002)  
to supply the exporters

**[01:14:17.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002003)  
so that they can actually move their products also.

**[01:14:21.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002004)  
And since ang produce nila maliit lang,

**[01:14:23.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002005)  
they can supply on a daily basis,

**[01:14:25.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002006)  
which is fine for a lot of these exporters.

**[01:14:28.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002007)  
We will continue to open doors for exporters.

**[01:14:31.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002008)  
Under this administration,

**[01:14:32.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002009)  
is set to make history and 23 free trade agreements.

**[01:14:37.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002010)  
This year alone,

**[01:14:38.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002011)  
we will hope to conclude the JPEPA,

**[01:14:41.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002012)  
and we have also seen the conclusion

**[01:14:43.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002013)  
in the European,

**[01:14:44.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002014)  
Philippine-European FTA.

**[01:14:46.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002015)  
We signed the Philippine-Chile FTA

**[01:14:49.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002016)  
that will open the South America market.

**[01:14:51.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002017)  
Then we also have the ASEAN trade in goods agreement.

**[01:14:55.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002018)  
So there are a lot of FTAs in place,

**[01:14:58.140] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002019)  
but we will end by

**[01:15:00.000] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002020)  
23 by this year

**[01:15:02.500] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002021)  
concluded.

**[01:15:04.260] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002022)  
Aside from market access, our exporters

**[01:15:06.580] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002023)  
must still connect with buyers,

**[01:15:08.540] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002024)  
showcase their products, and secure orders.

**[01:15:10.960] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002025)  
We are proposing an

**[01:15:12.460] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002026)  
additional budget of 100 million

**[01:15:14.340] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002027)  
that will allow more Philippine companies

**[01:15:16.480] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002028)  
to participate in international trade

**[01:15:18.440] DTI Secretary Maria Cristina Aldeguer-Roque** (asr-002029)  
shows where actual business
